# In pursuance of sub-section (3) of Section 19 of the Chartered Accountants Act, 1949, the list of Members of the Institute of Chartered Accountants of India as on the 1st day of April 1954, is hereby published for general information

NOTIFICATION New Delhi, the 17th June 1954 No. 10-CA(l)/53.—In pursuance of sub-section

(3) of Section 19 of the Chartered Accountants Act, 1949, the list of Members of the Institute of Chartered Accountants of India as on the 1st day of April 1954, is hereby published for general information:—

EXPLANATORY NOTES There are two classes of members, viz , Fellows and Associates. Fellows and Associates are entitled to use the letters "F.C.A." and "A.C.A." respectively after their names. 'F' after a member's name denotes Fellow and 'A' Associate. The date following 'F' is the date of election to Fellowship and the date following 'A' is the date of admission to Associateship, The number in brackets at the end indicates" Membership Number of the member concerned.

A dagger ( ) before a member's name indicates that the member is not in practice.

The names of Chartered Accountants firms only are given in bold. In brackets are shown the names of the partners only of the respective Chartered Accountants firms.

Members who are employed by a firm of Chartered Accountants are described as 'with' that firm.

Members' who are employed by a concern other than a firm of Chartered Accountants are described as 'C/o' that concern.

The first list has been arranged alphabetically and the second list has been arranged topographically according to Regions.
